=== Project cssNew folder/README.md ===
# ESP32 Dual-Axis Solar Tracker Dashboard

Modern, industrial-grade IoT telemetry dashboard built with **React + TypeScript + Vite + Tailwind CSS**, ready to push to **GitHub** and deploy on **Vercel**.

## Features

- **Dual-Axis Gimbal & Azimuth Compass**: Real-time SVG gauges for **Pan (`0Â°â€“180Â°`)** and **Tilt (`0Â°â€“180Â°`)** with live PWM microsecond conversion (`500â€“2500 Âµs`) and interactive servo override sliders in **Manual Override** mode (`POST /api/control`).
- **4-Quadrant LDR Matrix**: Displays **Top-Left**, **Top-Right**, **Bottom-Left**, and **Bottom-Right** 12-bit ADC readings (`0â€“4095`), percentage irradiance, dynamic solar glow intensity, and a central differential sun-pull crosshair (`Î”X` / `Î”Y`) with deadband lock detection.
- **Environmental Telemetry Cards**: Live SVG area sparkline trend charts for **Temperature (`Â°C`)**, **Humidity (`% RH`)**, and **Gas / Air Quality (`PPM`)** with automatic **Safe / Elevated / Hazard** threshold badges.
- **Demo Simulation + Live ESP32 API**: Runs out of the box on Vercel with an interactive **Demo Simulation** mode (Solar Sweep, Clear Noon, Cloudy Drift, Gas Alert Test) and includes an **ESP32 API** modal to connect your ESP32 hardware endpoint.

---

## Local Development

```bash
npm install
npm run dev
```

To create a production build locally:

```bash
npm run build
```

---

## Upload to GitHub & Deploy on Vercel

1. **Create a GitHub repository** (e.g., `esp32-solar-tracker-ui`).
2. **Push this project to GitHub**:
   ```bash
   git init
   git add .
   git commit -m "Initial commit: ESP32 Dual-Axis Solar Tracker Dashboard"
   git branch -M main
   git remote add origin https://github.com/<your-username>/esp32-solar-tracker-ui.git
   git push -u origin main
   ```
3. **Deploy on Vercel**:
   - Go to [vercel.com/new](https://vercel.com/new) and import your GitHub repository.
   - Vercel automatically detects **Vite** (`Build Command: npm run build`, `Output Directory: dist`).
   - Click **Deploy**.

---

## ESP32 HTTP API Contract

### `GET /api/telemetry`
```json
{
  "temperature": 29.4,
  "humidity": 54.2,
  "gasPpm": 215,
  "panAngle": 92,
  "tiltAngle": 68,
  "mode": "auto",
  "ldr": { "tl": 3420, "tr": 3390, "bl": 3180, "br": 3210 },
  "rssi": -58,
  "uptimeSec": 1420
}
```

### `POST /api/control`
```json
{
  "mode": "manual",
  "panAngle": 110,
  "tiltAngle": 75
}
```

> **Mixed-Content Note**: When hosted on Vercel (`https://`), browsers block direct `http://192.168.x.x` requests. For live control from Vercel, expose your ESP32 via an HTTPS tunnel (e.g., Cloudflare Tunnel or ngrok) or run the dashboard locally via `npm run dev`.
=== Project cssNew folder/esp32/solar_tracker_esp32.ino ===
/*
 * ESP32 Dual-Axis Solar Tracker Firmware (Reference Sketch)
 * Compatible with the ESP32 Solar Tracker Dashboard UI
 *
 * Endpoints:
 *   GET  /api/telemetry  -> Returns JSON telemetry (temp, humidity, gasPpm, panAngle, tiltAngle, mode, ldr)
 *   POST /api/control    -> Accepts JSON { "mode": "auto"|"manual", "panAngle": 0..180, "tiltAngle": 0..180 }
 *
 * Required Libraries (Arduino IDE / PlatformIO):
 *   - WiFi.h, WebServer.h (ESP32 core)
 *   - ESP32Servo
 *   - DHT sensor library (Adafruit)
 *   - ArduinoJson (v6+)
 */

#include <WiFi.h>
#include <WebServer.h>
#include <ESP32Servo.h>
#include <DHT.h>
#include <ArduinoJson.h>

// --- Wi-Fi Credentials ---
const char* WIFI_SSID = "YOUR_WIFI_SSID";
const char* WIFI_PASS = "YOUR_WIFI_PASSWORD";

// --- Pin Definitions ---
#define PIN_LDR_TL 34
#define PIN_LDR_TR 35
#define PIN_LDR_BL 32
#define PIN_LDR_BR 33
#define PIN_GAS_MQ 36
#define PIN_DHT    4
#define PIN_SERVO_PAN  18
#define PIN_SERVO_TILT 19

#define DHTTYPE DHT22
#define LDR_DEADBAND 150

DHT dht(PIN_DHT, DHTTYPE);
Servo panServo;
Servo tiltServo;
WebServer server(80);

String trackerMode = "auto";
int panAngle = 90;
int tiltAngle = 70;

int ldrTL = 0, ldrTR = 0, ldrBL = 0, ldrBR = 0;
float temperature = 28.0;
float humidity = 55.0;
int gasPpm = 200;

unsigned long lastTrackMs = 0;
unsigned long lastDhtMs = 0;

void sendCorsHeaders() {
  server.sendHeader("Access-Control-Allow-Origin", "*");
  server.sendHeader("Access-Control-Allow-Methods", "GET, POST, OPTIONS");
  server.sendHeader("Access-Control-Allow-Headers", "Content-Type, Accept");
}

void handleOptions() {
  sendCorsHeaders();
  server.send(204);
}

void handleTelemetry() {
  sendCorsHeaders();
  StaticJsonDocument<384> doc;
  doc["temperature"] = round(temperature * 10.0) / 10.0;
  doc["humidity"] = round(humidity * 10.0) / 10.0;
  doc["gasPpm"] = gasPpm;
  doc["panAngle"] = panAngle;
  doc["tiltAngle"] = tiltAngle;
  doc["mode"] = trackerMode;
  doc["rssi"] = WiFi.RSSI();
  doc["uptimeSec"] = millis() / 1000;

  JsonObject ldr = doc.createNestedObject("ldr");
  ldr["tl"] = ldrTL;
  ldr["tr"] = ldrTR;
  ldr["bl"] = ldrBL;
  ldr["br"] = ldrBR;

  String json;
  serializeJson(doc, json);
  server.send(200, "application/json", json);
}

void handleControl() {
  sendCorsHeaders();
  if (!server.hasArg("plain")) {
    server.send(400, "application/json", "{\"error\":\"Missing body\"}");
    return;
  }

  StaticJsonDocument<256> doc;
  DeserializationError err = deserializeJson(doc, server.arg("plain"));
  if (err) {
    server.send(400, "application/json", "{\"error\":\"Invalid JSON\"}");
    return;
  }

  if (doc.containsKey("mode")) {
    String m = doc["mode"].as<String>();
    if (m == "auto" || m == "manual") trackerMode = m;
  }
  if (trackerMode == "manual") {
    if (doc.containsKey("panAngle")) {
      panAngle = constrain(doc["panAngle"].as<int>(), 0, 180);
      panServo.write(panAngle);
    }
    if (doc.containsKey("tiltAngle")) {
      tiltAngle = constrain(doc["tiltAngle"].as<int>(), 0, 180);
      tiltServo.write(tiltAngle);
    }
  }

  server.send(200, "application/json", "{\"status\":\"ok\"}");
}

void setup() {
  Serial.begin(115200);
  dht.begin();

  panServo.attach(PIN_SERVO_PAN, 500, 2500);
  tiltServo.attach(PIN_SERVO_TILT, 500, 2500);
  panServo.write(panAngle);
  tiltServo.write(tiltAngle);

  WiFi.begin(WIFI_SSID, WIFI_PASS);
  Serial.print("Connecting to Wi-Fi");
  while (WiFi.status() != WL_CONNECTED) {
    delay(400);
    Serial.print(".");
  }
  Serial.println();
  Serial.print("ESP32 IP: ");
  Serial.println(WiFi.localIP());

  server.on("/api/telemetry", HTTP_OPTIONS, handleOptions);
  server.on("/api/telemetry", HTTP_GET, handleTelemetry);
  server.on("/api/control", HTTP_OPTIONS, handleOptions);
  server.on("/api/control", HTTP_POST, handleControl);
  server.begin();
}

void loop() {
  server.handleClient();

  unsigned long now = millis();

  // Read LDRs + Gas ADC
  ldrTL = analogRead(PIN_LDR_TL);
  ldrTR = analogRead(PIN_LDR_TR);
  ldrBL = analogRead(PIN_LDR_BL);
  ldrBR = analogRead(PIN_LDR_BR);
  int rawGas = analogRead(PIN_GAS_MQ);
  gasPpm = map(rawGas, 0, 4095, 100, 1500);

  // Read DHT every 2s
  if (now - lastDhtMs >= 2000) {
    lastDhtMs = now;
    float t = dht.readTemperature();
    float h = dht.readHumidity();
    if (!isnan(t)) temperature = t;
    if (!isnan(h)) humidity = h;
  }

  // Closed-loop auto tracking every 80ms
  if (trackerMode == "auto" && (now - lastTrackMs >= 80)) {
    lastTrackMs = now;
    int avgTop = (ldrTL + ldrTR) / 2;
    int avgBot = (ldrBL + ldrBR) / 2;
    int avgLeft = (ldrTL + ldrBL) / 2;
    int avgRight = (ldrTR + ldrBR) / 2;

    int deltaX = avgRight - avgLeft;
    int deltaY = avgTop - avgBot;

    if (abs(deltaX) > LDR_DEADBAND) {
      panAngle = constrain(panAngle + (deltaX > 0 ? 1 : -1), 0, 180);
      panServo.write(panAngle);
    }
    if (abs(deltaY) > LDR_DEADBAND) {
      tiltAngle = constrain(tiltAngle + (deltaY > 0 ? 1 : -1), 0, 180);
      tiltServo.write(tiltAngle);
    }
  }
}

=== Project cssNew folder/src/App.tsx ===
import { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { AlertTriangle, Clock, Database } from 'lucide-react';
import { EnvironmentalCards } from './components/EnvironmentalCards';
import { Esp32ApiModal } from './components/Esp32ApiModal';
import { GimbalCompassCard } from './components/GimbalCompassCard';
import { HeaderBar } from './components/HeaderBar';
import { LdrMatrixCard } from './components/LdrMatrixCard';
import {
  ApiConfig,
  ConnectionState,
  SimulationScenario,
  TelemetryHistoryPoint,
  TelemetryPacket,
  TrackerMode,
} from './types/telemetry';
import { fetchEsp32Telemetry, sendEsp32Control } from './utils/esp32Api';
import {
  createInitialSimulationState,
  SimulationState,
  stepSimulation,
} from './utils/simulation';

const MAX_HISTORY_POINTS = 30;
const STORAGE_KEY = 'esp32_solar_tracker_api_config';

function loadSavedApiConfig(): ApiConfig {
  try {
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY);
    if (raw) {
      const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
      return {
        baseUrl: typeof parsed.baseUrl === 'string' ? parsed.baseUrl : 'http://192.168.1.50',
        pollIntervalMs: Number(parsed.pollIntervalMs) || 1000,
      };
    }
  } catch {
    // Ignore storage access errors
  }
  return { baseUrl: 'http://192.168.1.50', pollIntervalMs: 1000 };
}

function pushHistory(
  prev: TelemetryHistoryPoint[],
  packet: TelemetryPacket
): TelemetryHistoryPoint[] {
  const next = [
    ...prev,
    {
      timestamp: packet.timestamp,
      temperature: packet.temperature,
      humidity: packet.humidity,
      gasPpm: packet.gasPpm,
    },
  ];
  return next.length > MAX_HISTORY_POINTS
    ? next.slice(next.length - MAX_HISTORY_POINTS)
    : next;
}

export default function App() {
  const initialSim = useRef<SimulationState>(createInitialSimulationState());
  const firstStep = useRef(stepSimulation(initialSim.current, 'solar_sweep', 1));

  const [demoMode, setDemoMode] = useState(true);
  const [scenario, setScenario] = useState<SimulationScenario>('solar_sweep');
  const [apiConfig, setApiConfig] = useState<ApiConfig>(loadSavedApiConfig);
  const [isApiModalOpen, setIsApiModalOpen] = useState(false);

  const [telemetry, setTelemetry] = useState<TelemetryPacket>(
    firstStep.current.packet
  );
  const [history, setHistory] = useState<TelemetryHistoryPoint[]>(() => [
    {
      timestamp: firstStep.current.packet.timestamp,
      temperature: firstStep.current.packet.temperature,
      humidity: firstStep.current.packet.humidity,
      gasPpm: firstStep.current.packet.gasPpm,
    },
  ]);

  const [mode, setMode] = useState<TrackerMode>('auto');
  const [manualPan, setManualPan] = useState(firstStep.current.packet.panAngle);
  const [manualTilt, setManualTilt] = useState(firstStep.current.packet.tiltAngle);

  const [connectionState, setConnectionState] =
    useState<ConnectionState>('simulation');
  const [lastError, setLastError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [commandStatus, setCommandStatus] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [packetCount, setPacketCount] = useState(1);

  const simRef = useRef<SimulationState>(firstStep.current.nextState);
  const controlTimerRef = useRef<number | null>(null);

  // Persist API config
  const handleSaveApiConfig = (next: ApiConfig, disableDemo?: boolean) => {
    setApiConfig(next);
    try {
      localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(next));
    } catch {
      // Ignore storage errors
    }
    if (disableDemo) {
      setDemoMode(false);
    }
  };

  // Close modal with Escape
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isApiModalOpen) return;
    const onKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.key === 'Escape') setIsApiModalOpen(false);
    };
    window.addEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
  }, [isApiModalOpen]);

  // Keep simulation state synced with user's mode & manual targets
  useEffect(() => {
    simRef.current = {
      ...simRef.current,
      mode,
      targetPan: manualPan,
      targetTilt: manualTilt,
    };
  }, [mode, manualPan, manualTilt]);

  // Telemetry polling loop (Demo Simulation vs Live ESP32)
  useEffect(() => {
    if (demoMode) {
      setConnectionState('simulation');
      setLastError(null);

      const interval = window.setInterval(() => {
        const { nextState, packet } = stepSimulation(
          simRef.current,
          scenario,
          apiConfig.pollIntervalMs / 1000
        );
        simRef.current = nextState;
        setTelemetry(packet);
        setHistory((prev) => pushHistory(prev, packet));
        setPacketCount((c) => c + 1);
      }, Math.max(400, apiConfig.pollIntervalMs));

      return () => window.clearInterval(interval);
    }

    let cancelled = false;
    setConnectionState('connecting');

    const poll = async () => {
      try {
        const packet = await fetchEsp32Telemetry(apiConfig.baseUrl);
        if (cancelled) return;
        setTelemetry(packet);
        setHistory((prev) => pushHistory(prev, packet));
        setPacketCount((c) => c + 1);
        setConnectionState('connected');
        setLastError(null);
      } catch (err) {
        if (cancelled) return;
        setConnectionState('error');
        setLastError(
          err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Unable to reach ESP32'
        );
      }
    };

    poll();
    const interval = window.setInterval(poll, Math.max(400, apiConfig.pollIntervalMs));

    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
      window.clearInterval(interval);
    };
  }, [demoMode, scenario, apiConfig]);

  const dispatchControl = useCallback(
    (nextMode: TrackerMode, pan: number, tilt: number, immediate = false) => {
      if (controlTimerRef.current) {
        window.clearTimeout(controlTimerRef.current);
      }

      const send = async () => {
        if (demoMode) {
          setCommandStatus(
            `SIM POST /api/control â†’ ${nextMode.toUpperCase()} (${pan}Â°, ${tilt}Â°)`
          );
          return;
        }
        try {
          setCommandStatus('Sending POST /api/control...');
          await sendEsp32Control(apiConfig.baseUrl, {
            mode: nextMode,
            panAngle: pan,
            tiltAngle: tilt,
          });
          setCommandStatus(`Synced ${nextMode.toUpperCase()} (${pan}Â°, ${tilt}Â°)`);
        } catch (err) {
          setCommandStatus(
            err instanceof Error ? `Err: ${err.message}` : 'Control failed'
          );
        }
      };

      if (immediate) {
        send();
      } else {
        controlTimerRef.current = window.setTimeout(send, 120);
      }
    },
    [demoMode, apiConfig.baseUrl]
  );

  const handleModeChange = (nextMode: TrackerMode) => {
    setMode(nextMode);
    if (nextMode === 'manual') {
      setManualPan(telemetry.panAngle);
      setManualTilt(telemetry.tiltAngle);
      dispatchControl('manual', telemetry.panAngle, telemetry.tiltAngle, true);
    } else {
      dispatchControl('auto', telemetry.panAngle, telemetry.tiltAngle, true);
    }
  };

  const handleManualPanChange = (pan: number) => {
    setManualPan(pan);
    dispatchControl('manual', pan, manualTilt);
  };

  const handleManualTiltChange = (tilt: number) => {
    setManualTilt(tilt);
    dispatchControl('manual', manualPan, tilt);
  };

  const handleApplyPreset = (pan: number, tilt: number) => {
    setManualPan(pan);
    setManualTilt(tilt);
    dispatchControl('manual', pan, tilt, true);
  };

  const isGasHazard = telemetry.gasPpm >= 800;

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen px-4 py-5 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
      <div className="mx-auto flex max-w-7xl flex-col gap-5">
        <HeaderBar
          demoMode={demoMode}
          onToggleDemo={setDemoMode}
          scenario={scenario}
          onChangeScenario={setScenario}
          mode={mode}
          onChangeMode={handleModeChange}
          connectionState={connectionState}
          lastError={lastError}
          rssi={telemetry.rssi}
          uptimeSec={telemetry.uptimeSec}
          onOpenApiModal={() => setIsApiModalOpen(true)}
        />

        {/* Hazard Alert Banner */}
        {isGasHazard && (
          <div
            role="alert"
            className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 rounded-xl border border-rose-500/50 bg-rose-950/50 px-4 py-3 text-xs text-rose-200 shadow-lg"
          >
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
              <AlertTriangle className="h-5 w-5 shrink-0 text-rose-400" />
              <div>
                <span className="font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-rose-300">
                  Air Quality Hazard Detected:
                </span>{' '}
                Gas concentration reached{' '}
                <span className="font-mono font-bold text-white">
                  {telemetry.gasPpm} PPM
                </span>{' '}
                (threshold â‰¥ 800 PPM). Inspect enclosure ventilation or MQ-135 sensor.
              </div>
            </div>
            <span className="rounded-md bg-rose-500/20 px-2 py-1 font-mono text-[11px] font-semibold text-rose-300">
              ALERT ACTIVE
            </span>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Primary Telemetry Grid: Gimbal/Compass + 4-Quadrant LDR Matrix */}
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-5 lg:grid-cols-2">
          <GimbalCompassCard
            panAngle={telemetry.panAngle}
            tiltAngle={telemetry.tiltAngle}
            manualPan={manualPan}
            manualTilt={manualTilt}
            mode={mode}
            onChangeManualPan={handleManualPanChange}
            onChangeManualTilt={handleManualTiltChange}
            onApplyPreset={handleApplyPreset}
            commandStatus={commandStatus}
          />

          <LdrMatrixCard ldr={telemetry.ldr} />
        </div>

        {/* Environmental Sparkline Cards */}
        <EnvironmentalCards
          temperature={telemetry.temperature}
          humidity={telemetry.humidity}
          gasPpm={telemetry.gasPpm}
          history={history}
        />

        {/* Diagnostics Footer */}
        <footer className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 rounded-xl border border-slate-800/80 bg-[#0c121e]/70 px-4 py-2.5 font-mono text-[11px] text-slate-400">
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-4">
            <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5">
              <Database className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-cyan-400" />
              PACKETS: <strong className="text-slate-200">{packetCount}</strong>
            </span>
            <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5">
              <Clock className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-amber-400" />
              LAST SYNC:{' '}
              <strong className="text-slate-200">
                {new Date(telemetry.timestamp).toLocaleTimeString()}
              </strong>
            </span>
          </div>
          <div>
            SOURCE:{' '}
            <strong className="text-slate-200">
              {demoMode
                ? `Demo Engine (${scenario.replace('_', ' ')})`
                : `${apiConfig.baseUrl}/api/telemetry`}
            </strong>
          </div>
        </footer>
      </div>

      <Esp32ApiModal
        isOpen={isApiModalOpen}
        config={apiConfig}
        demoMode={demoMode}
        onClose={() => setIsApiModalOpen(false)}
        onSaveConfig={handleSaveApiConfig}
      />
    </div>
  );
}
=== Project cssNew folder/src/components/EnvironmentalCards.tsx ===
import { ReactNode } from 'react';
import { Droplets, Flame, Thermometer } from 'lucide-react';
import { GasStatusLevel, TelemetryHistoryPoint } from '../types/telemetry';

interface EnvironmentalCardsProps {
  temperature: number;
  humidity: number;
  gasPpm: number;
  history: TelemetryHistoryPoint[];
}

interface BadgeStyle {
  label: string;
  classes: string;
}

interface SparklineCardProps {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  sensorModel: string;
  value: string;
  unit: string;
  icon: ReactNode;
  accentColor: string;
  badge: BadgeStyle;
  series: number[];
  decimals?: number;
}

function getGasStatus(ppm: number): { level: GasStatusLevel; badge: BadgeStyle } {
  if (ppm >= 800) {
    return {
      level: 'Hazard',
      badge: {
        label: 'HAZARD',
        classes: 'border-rose-500/50 bg-rose-500/15 text-rose-300 animate-pulse',
      },
    };
  }
  if (ppm >= 400) {
    return {
      level: 'Elevated',
      badge: {
        label: 'ELEVATED',
        classes: 'border-amber-500/40 bg-amber-500/15 text-amber-300',
      },
    };
  }
  return {
    level: 'Safe',
    badge: {
      label: 'SAFE',
      classes: 'border-emerald-500/40 bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-300',
    },
  };
}

function getTempBadge(temp: number): BadgeStyle {
  if (temp >= 40) {
    return {
      label: 'HIGH HEAT',
      classes: 'border-rose-500/40 bg-rose-500/15 text-rose-300',
    };
  }
  if (temp >= 33) {
    return {
      label: 'WARM',
      classes: 'border-amber-500/40 bg-amber-500/10 text-amber-300',
    };
  }
  return {
    label: 'OPTIMAL',
    classes: 'border-emerald-500/40 bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-300',
  };
}

function getHumidityBadge(rh: number): BadgeStyle {
  if (rh >= 75) {
    return {
      label: 'HIGH RH',
      classes: 'border-cyan-500/40 bg-cyan-500/15 text-cyan-300',
    };
  }
  if (rh <= 30) {
    return {
      label: 'DRY AIR',
      classes: 'border-amber-500/40 bg-amber-500/10 text-amber-300',
    };
  }
  return {
    label: 'NOMINAL',
    classes: 'border-emerald-500/40 bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-300',
  };
}

function TelemetrySparklineCard({
  id,
  title,
  sensorModel,
  value,
  unit,
  icon,
  accentColor,
  badge,
  series,
  decimals = 1,
}: SparklineCardProps) {
  const width = 280;
  const height = 64;
  const padY = 6;

  const safeSeries = series.length > 1 ? series : [0, 0];
  const minVal = Math.min(...safeSeries);
  const maxVal = Math.max(...safeSeries);
  const span = Math.max(maxVal - minVal, 0.5);

  const points = safeSeries.map((v, idx) => {
    const x = (idx / (safeSeries.length - 1)) * width;
    const y = height - padY - ((v - minVal) / span) * (height - padY * 2);
    return { x, y };
  });

  const linePath = points
    .map((pt, i) => `${i === 0 ? 'M' : 'L'} ${pt.x.toFixed(1)} ${pt.y.toFixed(1)}`)
    .join(' ');
  const areaPath = `${linePath} L ${width} ${height} L 0 ${height} Z`;
  const lastPt = points[points.length - 1];

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col justify-between rounded-2xl border border-slate-800/90 bg-[#0c121e]/90 p-5 shadow-xl">
      <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-2">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
          <div
            className="flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-xl border border-slate-700/80 bg-slate-900"
            style={{ color: accentColor }}
          >
            {icon}
          </div>
          <div>
            <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-100">{title}</h3>
            <p className="font-mono text-[11px] text-slate-500">{sensorModel}</p>
          </div>
        </div>

        <span
          className={`rounded-full border px-2.5 py-0.5 font-mono text-[11px] font-semibold ${badge.classes}`}
        >
          {badge.label}
        </span>
      </div>

      <div className="mt-4 flex items-baseline justify-between">
        <div className="font-mono">
          <span className="text-3xl font-bold tabular-nums text-slate-100">
            {value}
          </span>
          <span className="ml-1.5 text-sm font-medium text-slate-400">
            {unit}
          </span>
        </div>

        <div className="text-right font-mono text-[11px] text-slate-400">
          <div>MIN {minVal.toFixed(decimals)}</div>
          <div>MAX {maxVal.toFixed(decimals)}</div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="mt-3 overflow-hidden rounded-xl border border-slate-800/70 bg-slate-950/70 px-2 pt-2">
        <svg viewBox={`0 0 ${width} ${height}`} className="h-16 w-full">
          <defs>
            <linearGradient id={`grad-${id}`} x1="0" y1="0" x2="0" y2="1">
              <stop offset="0%" stopColor={accentColor} stopOpacity="0.35" />
              <stop offset="100%" stopColor={accentColor} stopOpacity="0.0" />
            </linearGradient>
          </defs>
          <path d={areaPath} fill={`url(#grad-${id})`} />
          <path
            d={linePath}
            fill="none"
            stroke={accentColor}
            strokeWidth="2.2"
            strokeLinecap="round"
            strokeLinejoin="round"
          />
          <circle
            cx={lastPt.x}
            cy={lastPt.y}
            r="3.2"
            fill={accentColor}
            stroke="#06090f"
            strokeWidth="1.5"
          />
        </svg>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export function EnvironmentalCards({
  temperature,
  humidity,
  gasPpm,
  history,
}: EnvironmentalCardsProps) {
  const gasStatus = getGasStatus(gasPpm);

  return (
    <section className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-5 md:grid-cols-3">
      <TelemetrySparklineCard
        id="temp"
        title="Temperature"
        sensorModel="DHT22 / BME280"
        value={temperature.toFixed(1)}
        unit="Â°C"
        icon={<Thermometer className="h-4 w-4" />}
        accentColor="#fb923c"
        badge={getTempBadge(temperature)}
        series={history.map((h) => h.temperature)}
        decimals={1}
      />

      <TelemetrySparklineCard
        id="humidity"
        title="Relative Humidity"
        sensorModel="DHT22 / BME280"
        value={humidity.toFixed(1)}
        unit="% RH"
        icon={<Droplets className="h-4 w-4" />}
        accentColor="#38bdf8"
        badge={getHumidityBadge(humidity)}
        series={history.map((h) => h.humidity)}
        decimals={1}
      />

      <TelemetrySparklineCard
        id="gas"
        title="Gas / Air Quality"
        sensorModel="MQ-135 / MQ-2"
        value={String(gasPpm)}
        unit="PPM"
        icon={<Flame className="h-4 w-4" />}
        accentColor={
          gasStatus.level === 'Hazard'
            ? '#f43f5e'
            : gasStatus.level === 'Elevated'
              ? '#f59e0b'
              : '#10b981'
        }
        badge={gasStatus.badge}
        series={history.map((h) => h.gasPpm)}
        decimals={0}
      />
    </section>
  );
}

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import {
  CheckCircle2,
  Copy,
  Globe,
  RefreshCw,
  Server,
  ShieldAlert,
  X,
} from 'lucide-react';
import { ApiConfig } from '../types/telemetry';
import { fetchEsp32Telemetry } from '../utils/esp32Api';

interface Esp32ApiModalProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  config: ApiConfig;
  demoMode: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  onSaveConfig: (next: ApiConfig, disableDemo?: boolean) => void;
}

const SAMPLE_TELEMETRY = `{
  "temperature": 29.4,
  "humidity": 54.2,
  "gasPpm": 215,
  "panAngle": 92,
  "tiltAngle": 68,
  "mode": "auto",
  "ldr": { "tl": 3420, "tr": 3390, "bl": 3180, "br": 3210 },
  "rssi": -58,
  "uptimeSec": 1420
}`;

const SAMPLE_CONTROL = `{
  "mode": "manual",
  "panAngle": 110,
  "tiltAngle": 75
}`;

export function Esp32ApiModal({
  isOpen,
  config,
  demoMode,
  onClose,
  onSaveConfig,
}: Esp32ApiModalProps) {
  const [baseUrl, setBaseUrl] = useState(config.baseUrl);
  const [pollIntervalMs, setPollIntervalMs] = useState(config.pollIntervalMs);
  const [testState, setTestState] = useState<{
    status: 'idle' | 'testing' | 'ok' | 'error';
    message: string;
  }>({ status: 'idle', message: '' });
  const [copied, setCopied] = useState<string | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (isOpen) {
      setBaseUrl(config.baseUrl);
      setPollIntervalMs(config.pollIntervalMs);
      setTestState({ status: 'idle', message: '' });
    }
  }, [isOpen, config]);

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  const handleTest = async () => {
    setTestState({ status: 'testing', message: 'Querying /api/telemetry...' });
    try {
      const packet = await fetchEsp32Telemetry(baseUrl);
      setTestState({
        status: 'ok',
        message: `Connected! Temp ${packet.temperature}Â°C, Pan ${packet.panAngle}Â°, Tilt ${packet.tiltAngle}Â°`,
      });
    } catch (err) {
      setTestState({
        status: 'error',
        message: err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Request failed',
      });
    }
  };

  const handleCopy = async (key: string, text: string) => {
    try {
      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(text);
      setCopied(key);
      window.setTimeout(() => setCopied(null), 1600);
    } catch {
      // Clipboard API unavailable in restricted contexts
    }
  };

  return (
    <div
      className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-black/75 p-4 backdrop-blur-sm"
      role="dialog"
      aria-modal="true"
      aria-labelledby="esp32-modal-title"
    >
      <div className="max-h-[90vh] w-full max-w-2xl overflow-y-auto rounded-2xl border border-slate-700/80 bg-[#0b111d] p-6 shadow-2xl">
        <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-4 border-b border-slate-800 pb-4">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <div className="flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-xl border border-cyan-500/30 bg-cyan-500/10 text-cyan-400">
              <Server className="h-5 w-5" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <h2
                id="esp32-modal-title"
                className="text-base font-semibold text-slate-100"
              >
                ESP32 HTTP Telemetry &amp; Control API
              </h2>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-400">
                Configure your ESP32 endpoint for live polling and PWM control
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onClose}
            aria-label="Close modal"
            className="rounded-lg p-1.5 text-slate-400 transition hover:bg-slate-800 hover:text-slate-100"
          >
            <X className="h-5 w-5" />
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="mt-5 space-y-5">
          {/* Endpoint Inputs */}
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-3">
            <div className="sm:col-span-2">
              <label
                htmlFor="esp32-url"
                className="block font-mono text-xs font-medium text-slate-300"
              >
                ESP32 Base URL
              </label>
              <div className="relative mt-1.5">
                <Globe className="pointer-events-none absolute left-3 top-2.5 h-4 w-4 text-slate-500" />
                <input
                  id="esp32-url"
                  type="url"
                  value={baseUrl}
                  onChange={(e) => setBaseUrl(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="http://192.168.1.50"
                  className="w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-700 bg-slate-950 py-2 pl-9 pr-3 font-mono text-xs text-slate-100 outline-none focus:border-cyan-500"
                />
              </div>
            </div>

            <div>
              <label
                htmlFor="poll-ms"
                className="block font-mono text-xs font-medium text-slate-300"
              >
                Poll Interval
              </label>
              <select
                id="poll-ms"
                value={pollIntervalMs}
                onChange={(e) => setPollIntervalMs(Number(e.target.value))}
                className="mt-1.5 w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-700 bg-slate-950 px-3 py-2 font-mono text-xs text-slate-100 outline-none focus:border-cyan-500"
              >
                <option value={500}>500 ms (2 Hz)</option>
                <option value={1000}>1000 ms (1 Hz)</option>
                <option value={2000}>2000 ms (0.5 Hz)</option>
                <option value={5000}>5000 ms</option>
              </select>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Connection Test Row */}
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 rounded-xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-950/70 p-3">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={handleTest}
              disabled={testState.status === 'testing'}
              className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-lg border border-cyan-500/40 bg-cyan-500/15 px-3 py-1.5 font-mono text-xs font-medium text-cyan-200 transition hover:bg-cyan-500/25 disabled:opacity-50"
            >
              <RefreshCw
                className={`h-3.5 w-3.5 ${testState.status === 'testing' ? 'animate-spin' : ''}`}
              />
              Test GET /api/telemetry
            </button>

            <div className="font-mono text-xs">
              {testState.status === 'ok' && (
                <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-emerald-300">
                  <CheckCircle2 className="h-4 w-4" />
                  {testState.message}
                </span>
              )}
              {testState.status === 'error' && (
                <span className="text-rose-400">{testState.message}</span>
              )}
              {testState.status === 'idle' && (
                <span className="text-slate-500">
                  Target: {baseUrl.replace(/\/+$/, '')}/api/telemetry
                </span>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* HTTPS Mixed Content Warning for Vercel */}
          <div className="flex items-start gap-3 rounded-xl border border-amber-500/30 bg-amber-500/10 p-3.5 text-xs text-amber-200/90">
            <ShieldAlert className="mt-0.5 h-4 w-4 shrink-0 text-amber-400" />
            <div className="space-y-1">
              <p className="font-semibold text-amber-300">
                Vercel HTTPS Mixed-Content Note
              </p>
              <p className="leading-relaxed text-slate-300">
                When hosted on Vercel (HTTPS), browsers block direct HTTP requests
                to local LAN IPs like <code className="text-amber-200">http://192.168.x.x</code>.
                For remote live control, expose your ESP32 via an HTTPS tunnel
                (Cloudflare Tunnel / ngrok) or test locally via <code className="text-amber-200">npm run dev</code>.
                Ensure your ESP32 sends CORS headers (<code className="text-amber-200">Access-Control-Allow-Origin: *</code>).
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* JSON Schema Reference */}
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
            <div className="rounded-xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-950/80 p-3">
              <div className="mb-2 flex items-center justify-between font-mono text-[11px]">
                <span className="font-semibold text-emerald-300">
                  GET /api/telemetry
                </span>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => handleCopy('telemetry', SAMPLE_TELEMETRY)}
                  className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-200"
                >
                  <Copy className="h-3 w-3" />
                  {copied === 'telemetry' ? 'Copied' : 'Copy'}
                </button>
              </div>
              <pre className="overflow-x-auto font-mono text-[11px] leading-relaxed text-slate-300">
                {SAMPLE_TELEMETRY}
              </pre>
            </div>

            <div className="rounded-xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-950/80 p-3">
              <div className="mb-2 flex items-center justify-between font-mono text-[11px]">
                <span className="font-semibold text-amber-300">
                  POST /api/control

                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => handleCopy('control', SAMPLE_CONTROL)}
                  className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-200"

                  <Copy className="h-3 w-3" />
                  {copied === 'control' ? 'Copied' : 'Copy'}
                </button>
              </div>

                {SAMPLE_CONTROL}

        <div className="mt-6 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-end gap-2.5 border-t border-slate-800 pt-4">

            >
              Save &amp; Switch to Live ESP32
            </button>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );

=== Project cssNew folder/src/components/GimbalCompassCard.tsx ===
import { Compass, Lock, RotateCw, Sliders, Unlock } from 'lucide-react';
import { TrackerMode } from '../types/telemetry';

interface GimbalCompassCardProps {
  panAngle: number;
  tiltAngle: number;
  manualPan: number;
  manualTilt: number;
  mode: TrackerMode;
  onChangeManualPan: (angle: number) => void;
  onChangeManualTilt: (angle: number) => void;
  onApplyPreset: (pan: number, tilt: number) => void;
  commandStatus: string | null;
}

const PRESETS = [
  { label: 'East Morning', pan: 30, tilt: 42 },
  { label: 'Solar Noon', pan: 90, tilt: 88 },
  { label: 'West Afternoon', pan: 150, tilt: 45 },
  { label: 'Stow Flat', pan: 90, tilt: 90 },
];

function angleToPwmUs(angle: number): number {
  return Math.round(500 + (angle / 180) * 2000);
}

export function GimbalCompassCard({
  panAngle,
  tiltAngle,
  manualPan,
  manualTilt,
  mode,
  onChangeManualPan,
  onChangeManualTilt,
  onApplyPreset,
  commandStatus,
}: GimbalCompassCardProps) {
  const isManual = mode === 'manual';

  // Compass arc geometry: 0 deg = East (left), 90 deg = South (top), 180 deg = West (right)
  const panRad = ((180 - panAngle) * Math.PI) / 180;
  const needleX = 110 + Math.cos(panRad) * 70;
  const needleY = 115 - Math.sin(panRad) * 70;

  // Tilt gimbal geometry: panel rotates around pivot (110, 108)
  const panelRotation = tiltAngle - 90;

  return (
    <section className="flex flex-col justify-between rounded-2xl border border-slate-800/90 bg-[#0c121e]/90 p-5 shadow-xl">
      <div>
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
            <div className="flex h-8 w-8 items-center justify-center rounded-lg border border-cyan-500/30 bg-cyan-500/10 text-cyan-400">
              <Compass className="h-4 w-4" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <h2 className="text-sm font-semibold tracking-wide text-slate-100 uppercase">
                Dual-Axis Gimbal &amp; Azimuth Compass
              </h2>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-400">
                Servo orientation telemetry &amp; PWM override
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>

          <span
            className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full border px-2.5 py-1 font-mono text-[11px] font-medium ${
              isManual
                ? 'border-amber-500/40 bg-amber-500/10 text-amber-300'
                : 'border-emerald-500/30 bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-300'
            }`}
          >
            {isManual ? (
              <>
                <Unlock className="h-3 w-3" /> MANUAL PWM
              </>
            ) : (
              <>
                <Lock className="h-3 w-3" /> AUTO CLOSED-LOOP
              </>
            )}
          </span>
        </div>

        {/* SVG Gauges Row */}
        <div className="mt-5 grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
          {/* Pan Azimuth Compass SVG */}
          <div className="flex flex-col items-center rounded-xl border border-slate-800/80 bg-slate-950/60 p-4">
            <div className="flex w-full items-center justify-between font-mono text-xs text-slate-400">
              <span>PAN AZIMUTH</span>
              <span className="text-cyan-400">{angleToPwmUs(panAngle)} Âµs</span>
            </div>

            <svg viewBox="0 0 220 140" className="mt-2 w-full max-w-[240px]">
              <defs>
                <linearGradient id="panArcGrad" x1="0%" y1="0%" x2="100%" y2="0%">
                  <stop offset="0%" stopColor="#06b6d4" />
                  <stop offset="50%" stopColor="#f59e0b" />
                  <stop offset="100%" stopColor="#f97316" />
                </linearGradient>
              </defs>

              {/* Outer semicircle track */}
              <path
                d="M 26 115 A 84 84 0 0 1 194 115"
                fill="none"
                stroke="#1e293b"
                strokeWidth="12"
                strokeLinecap="round"
              />
              {/* Active swept arc */}
              <path
                d={`M 26 115 A 84 84 0 0 1 ${110 + Math.cos(panRad) * 84} ${115 - Math.sin(panRad) * 84}`}
                fill="none"
                stroke="url(#panArcGrad)"
                strokeWidth="12"
                strokeLinecap="round"
              />

              {/* Degree ticks */}
              {[0, 45, 90, 135, 180].map((deg) => {
                const rad = ((180 - deg) * Math.PI) / 180;
                const x1 = 110 + Math.cos(rad) * 66;
                const y1 = 115 - Math.sin(rad) * 66;
                const x2 = 110 + Math.cos(rad) * 74;
                const y2 = 115 - Math.sin(rad) * 74;
                return (
                  <line
                    key={deg}
                    x1={x1}
                    y1={y1}
                    x2={x2}
                    y2={y2}
                    stroke="#64748b"
                    strokeWidth="1.5"
                  />
                );
              })}

              {/* Compass labels */}
              <text x="14" y="132" fill="#94a3b8" fontSize="10" fontFamily="monospace">
                E 0Â°
              </text>
              <text x="95" y="20" fill="#94a3b8" fontSize="10" fontFamily="monospace">
                S 90Â°
              </text>
              <text x="174" y="132" fill="#94a3b8" fontSize="10" fontFamily="monospace">
                180Â° W
              </text>

              {/* Needle */}
              <line
                x1="110"
                y1="115"
                x2={needleX}
                y2={needleY}
                stroke="#38bdf8"
                strokeWidth="3"
                strokeLinecap="round"
              />
              <circle cx="110" cy="115" r="6" fill="#0f172a" stroke="#38bdf8" strokeWidth="2.5" />

import {
  Activity,
  Cpu,
  Radio,
  Settings2,
  Sliders,
  Sun,
  Wifi,
  WifiOff,
} from 'lucide-react';
import {
  ConnectionState,
  SimulationScenario,
  TrackerMode,
} from '../types/telemetry';

interface HeaderBarProps {
  demoMode: boolean;
  onToggleDemo: (enabled: boolean) => void;
  scenario: SimulationScenario;
  onChangeScenario: (scenario: SimulationScenario) => void;
  mode: TrackerMode;
  onChangeMode: (mode: TrackerMode) => void;
  connectionState: ConnectionState;
  lastError: string | null;
  rssi: number;
  uptimeSec: number;
  onOpenApiModal: () => void;
}

function formatUptime(totalSeconds: number): string {
  const hrs = Math.floor(totalSeconds / 3600);
  const mins = Math.floor((totalSeconds % 3600) / 60);
  const secs = totalSeconds % 60;
  return `${String(hrs).padStart(2, '0')}:${String(mins).padStart(2, '0')}:${String(secs).padStart(2, '0')}`;
}

export function HeaderBar({
  demoMode,
  onToggleDemo,
  scenario,
  onChangeScenario,
  mode,
  onChangeMode,
  connectionState,
  lastError,
  rssi,
  uptimeSec,
  onOpenApiModal,
}: HeaderBarProps) {
  const statusConfig = {
    simulation: {
      label: 'DEMO SIMULATION',
      dot: 'bg-amber-400',
      badge: 'border-amber-500/30 bg-amber-500/10 text-amber-300',
    },
    connected: {
      label: 'ESP32 ONLINE',
      dot: 'bg-emerald-400',
      badge: 'border-emerald-500/30 bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-300',
    },
    connecting: {
      label: 'SYNCING...',
      dot: 'bg-cyan-400',
      badge: 'border-cyan-500/30 bg-cyan-500/10 text-cyan-300',
    },
    error: {
      label: 'LINK OFFLINE',
      dot: 'bg-rose-500',
      badge: 'border-rose-500/30 bg-rose-500/10 text-rose-300',
    },
  }[connectionState];

  return (
    <header className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-800/90 bg-[#0c121e]/90 p-4 shadow-2xl backdrop-blur-md">
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-4 xl:flex-row xl:items-center xl:justify-between">
        {/* Brand + Live Link Status */}
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3.5">
          <div className="flex h-11 w-11 items-center justify-center rounded-xl border border-amber-500/30 bg-gradient-to-br from-amber-500/20 to-orange-500/5 text-amber-400 shadow-inner">
            <Sun className="h-6 w-6" />
          </div>
          <div>
            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2.5">
              <h1 className="text-lg font-semibold tracking-tight text-slate-100 sm:text-xl">
                ESP32 Dual-Axis Solar Tracker
              </h1>
              <span
                className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full border px-2.5 py-0.5 font-mono text-[11px] font-medium ${statusConfig.badge}`}
              >
                <span className="relative flex h-2 w-2">
                  <span
                    className={`absolute inline-flex h-full w-full animate-ping rounded-full opacity-75 ${statusConfig.dot}`}
                  />
                  <span
                    className={`relative inline-flex h-2 w-2 rounded-full ${statusConfig.dot}`}
                  />
                </span>
                {statusConfig.label}
              </span>
            </div>
            <div className="mt-1 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-x-4 gap-y-1 font-mono text-xs text-slate-400">
              <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5">
                <Cpu className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-cyan-400" />
                ESP32-WROOM-32
              </span>
              <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5">
                {connectionState === 'error' ? (
                  <WifiOff className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-rose-400" />
                ) : (
                  <Wifi className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-emerald-400" />
                )}
                RSSI {rssi} dBm
              </span>
              <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5">
                <Activity className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-amber-400" />
                UP {formatUptime(uptimeSec)}
              </span>
              {lastError && !demoMode && (
                <span className="text-rose-400">Err: {lastError}</span>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Control Toolbar */}
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2.5">
          {/* Tracking Mode Switch */}
          <div className="flex items-center rounded-xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-950/80 p-1">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => onChangeMode('auto')}
              className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-lg px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium transition ${
                mode === 'auto'
                  ? 'bg-emerald-500/20 text-emerald-300 shadow-sm ring-1 ring-emerald-500/40'
                  : 'text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-200'
              }`}
            >
              <Radio className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
              Auto Track
            </button>
            <button

              className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-lg px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium transition ${

            <span className="text-xs font-medium text-slate-300">
              Demo Sim

            <button
              type="button"

              aria-checked={demoMode}

              className={`relative inline-flex h-5 w-9 shrink-0 cursor-pointer items-center rounded-full border transition-colors ${

                  demoMode ? 'translate-x-4' : 'translate-x-0.5'

              <select
                aria-label="Simulation scenario"
                value={scenario}
                onChange={(e) =>
                  onChangeScenario(e.target.value as SimulationScenario)
                }
                className="ml-1 rounded-md border border-slate-700/80 bg-slate-900 px-2 py-0.5 font-mono text-[11px] text-amber-200 outline-none focus:border-amber-500"
              >
                <option value="solar_sweep">Solar Sweep</option>
                <option value="clear_noon">Clear Noon</option>

                <option value="gas_alert">Gas Alert Test</option>
              </select>
            )}
          </div>

          {/* ESP32 API Modal Button */}
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onOpenApiModal}
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-xl border border-cyan-500/30 bg-cyan-500/10 px-3.5 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-cyan-300 transition hover:border-cyan-400/60 hover:bg-cyan-500/20"
          >
            <Settings2 className="h-4 w-4" />
            ESP32 API
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </header>
  );
}
=== Project cssNew folder/src/components/LdrMatrixCard.tsx ===
import { Crosshair, SunMedium } from 'lucide-react';
import { LdrQuadrants } from '../types/telemetry';

interface LdrMatrixCardProps {
  ldr: LdrQuadrants;
}

interface QuadrantMeta {
  key: keyof LdrQuadrants;
  label: string;
  pinHint: string;
}

const QUADRANTS: QuadrantMeta[] = [
  { key: 'tl', label: 'Top-Left (TL)', pinHint: 'GPIO34 / ADC1_CH6' },
  { key: 'tr', label: 'Top-Right (TR)', pinHint: 'GPIO35 / ADC1_CH7' },
  { key: 'bl', label: 'Bottom-Left (BL)', pinHint: 'GPIO32 / ADC1_CH4' },
  { key: 'br', label: 'Bottom-Right (BR)', pinHint: 'GPIO33 / ADC1_CH5' },
];

export function LdrMatrixCard({ ldr }: LdrMatrixCardProps) {
  const avgRight = (ldr.tr + ldr.br) / 2;
  const avgLeft = (ldr.tl + ldr.bl) / 2;
  const avgTop = (ldr.tl + ldr.tr) / 2;
  const avgBottom = (ldr.bl + ldr.br) / 2;

  const deltaX = Math.round(avgRight - avgLeft);
  const deltaY = Math.round(avgTop - avgBottom);
  const deadband = 150;
  const isLocked = Math.abs(deltaX) <= deadband && Math.abs(deltaY) <= deadband;

  // Map differential (-2000..+2000) into reticle coordinates (-36..+36)
  const clamp = (v: number, min: number, max: number) =>
    Math.min(max, Math.max(min, v));
  const dotX = 60 + clamp((deltaX / 1600) * 38, -40, 40);
  const dotY = 60 - clamp((deltaY / 1600) * 38, -40, 40);

  const avgIrradiancePct = (
    ((ldr.tl + ldr.tr + ldr.bl + ldr.br) / (4 * 4095)) *
    100
  ).toFixed(1);

  return (
    <section className="flex flex-col justify-between rounded-2xl border border-slate-800/90 bg-[#0c121e]/90 p-5 shadow-xl">
      <div>
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
            <div className="flex h-8 w-8 items-center justify-center rounded-lg border border-amber-500/30 bg-amber-500/10 text-amber-400">
              <SunMedium className="h-4 w-4" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <h2 className="text-sm font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-100">
                4-Quadrant LDR Matrix
              </h2>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-400">
                12-bit ADC irradiance (0â€“4095) &amp; sun-pull differential
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>

          <span className="rounded-full border border-slate-700 bg-slate-900 px-2.5 py-1 font-mono text-xs text-amber-300">
            AVG {avgIrradiancePct}%
          </span>
        </div>

        {/* 2x2 Quadrant Grid + Central Crosshair Overlay */}
        <div className="relative mt-5 grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
          {QUADRANTS.map((q) => {
            const raw = ldr[q.key];
            const ratio = Math.min(1, Math.max(0, raw / 4095));
            const pct = (ratio * 100).toFixed(1);
            const glowAlpha = (0.06 + ratio * 0.28).toFixed(2);
            const borderAlpha = (0.2 + ratio * 0.55).toFixed(2);

            return (
              <div
                key={q.key}
                style={{
                  backgroundImage: `radial-gradient(circle at 50% 35%, rgba(245, 158, 11, ${glowAlpha}), rgba(2, 6, 23, 0.85) 75%)`,
                  borderColor: `rgba(245, 158, 11, ${borderAlpha})`,
                }}
                className="flex flex-col justify-between rounded-xl border bg-slate-950/80 p-3.5 transition-all duration-300"
              >
                <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-2">
                  <div>
                    <div className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-200">
                      {q.label}
                    </div>
                    <div className="font-mono text-[10px] text-slate-500">
                      {q.pinHint}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <span className="rounded-md bg-amber-500/15 px-1.5 py-0.5 font-mono text-[11px] font-semibold text-amber-300">
                    {pct}%
                  </span>
                </div>

                <div className="mt-4">
                  <div className="flex items-baseline justify-between font-mono">
                    <span className="text-2xl font-bold tabular-nums text-slate-100">
                      {raw}
                    </span>
                    <span className="text-[11px] text-slate-500">/ 4095</span>
                  </div>
                  <div className="mt-2 h-1.5 w-full overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-slate-800/90">
                    <div
                      className="h-full rounded-full bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-500 to-yellow-300 transition-all duration-300"
                      style={{ width: `${pct}%` }}
                    />
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Central Differential Sun-Pull Crosshair */}
      <div className="mt-4 flex flex-col items-center gap-4 rounded-xl border border-slate-800/80 bg-slate-950/75 p-4 sm:flex-row sm:justify-between">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
          <svg
            viewBox="0 0 120 120"
            className="h-24 w-24 shrink-0 rounded-full border border-slate-800 bg-[#05080f]"
          >
            {/* Crosshair axes */}
            <line x1="60" y1="8" x2="60" y2="112" stroke="#1e293b" strokeWidth="1" />
            <line x1="8" y1="60" x2="112" y2="60" stroke="#1e293b" strokeWidth="1" />
            {/* Outer ring & deadband ring */}
            <circle cx="60" cy="60" r="44" fill="none" stroke="#1e293b" strokeWidth="1" />
            <circle
              cx="60"
              cy="60"
              r="11"
              fill={isLocked ? 'rgba(16,185,129,0.12)' : 'rgba(245,158,11,0.06)'}
              stroke={isLocked ? '#10b981' : '#475569'}
              strokeWidth="1"
              strokeDasharray="2 2"
            />
            {/* Pull vector line */}
            <line
              x1="60"
              y1="60"
              x2={dotX}
              y2={dotY}
              stroke={isLocked ? '#10b981' : '#f59e0b'}
              strokeWidth="1.75"
            />
            {/* Sun pull dot */}
            <circle
              cx={dotX}
              cy={dotY}
              r="5.5"
              fill={isLocked ? '#34d399' : '#fbbf24'}
              stroke="#06090f"
              strokeWidth="1.5"
            />
          </svg>

          <div>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-300">
              <Crosshair className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-amber-400" />
              Sun-Pull Vector
            </div>
            <div className="mt-1">
              <span
                className={`inline-flex items-center rounded-md border px-2 py-0.5 font-mono text-[11px] font-semibold ${
                  isLocked
                    ? 'border-emerald-500/40 bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-300'
                    : 'border-amber-500/40 bg-amber-500/10 text-amber-300'
                }`}
              >
                {isLocked ? 'LOCKED ON SUN (Â±150)' : 'TRACKING DIFFERENTIAL'}
              </span>
            </div>
            <p className="mt-1.5 font-mono text-[11px] text-slate-400">
              Deadband Â±{deadband} ADC counts
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="grid w-full grid-cols-2 gap-2.5 sm:w-auto">
          <div className="rounded-lg border border-slate-800 bg-slate-900/70 px-3 py-2 font-mono">
            <div className="text-[10px] uppercase text-slate-400">Î”X (Pan Err)</div>
            <div
              className={`mt-0.5 text-base font-bold tabular-nums ${
                Math.abs(deltaX) <= deadband ? 'text-emerald-300' : 'text-cyan-300'
              }`}
            >
              {deltaX >= 0 ? `+${deltaX}` : deltaX}
            </div>
          </div>
          <div className="rounded-lg border border-slate-800 bg-slate-900/70 px-3 py-2 font-mono">
            <div className="text-[10px] uppercase text-slate-400">Î”Y (Tilt Err)</div>
            <div
              className={`mt-0.5 text-base font-bold tabular-nums ${
                Math.abs(deltaY) <= deadband ? 'text-emerald-300' : 'text-amber-300'
              }`}
            >
              {deltaY >= 0 ? `+${deltaY}` : deltaY}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

export type TrackerMode = 'auto' | 'manual';

export type SimulationScenario =
  | 'solar_sweep'
  | 'clear_noon'
  | 'cloudy_drift'
  | 'gas_alert';

export interface LdrQuadrants {
  tl: number; // Top-Left 12-bit ADC (0 - 4095)
  tr: number; // Top-Right 12-bit ADC (0 - 4095)
  bl: number; // Bottom-Left 12-bit ADC (0 - 4095)
  br: number; // Bottom-Right 12-bit ADC (0 - 4095)
}

export interface TelemetryPacket {
  temperature: number; // Celsius
  humidity: number; // Relative Humidity %
  gasPpm: number; // Air quality / gas PPM
  panAngle: number; // 0 - 180 degrees
  tiltAngle: number; // 0 - 180 degrees
  mode: TrackerMode;
  ldr: LdrQuadrants;
  rssi: number; // Wi-Fi dBm
  uptimeSec: number;
  timestamp: number;
}

export interface TelemetryHistoryPoint {
  timestamp: number;
  temperature: number;
  humidity: number;
  gasPpm: number;
}

export interface ApiConfig {
  baseUrl: string;
  pollIntervalMs: number;
}

export interface ControlCommand {
  mode: TrackerMode;
  panAngle: number;
  tiltAngle: number;
}

export type ConnectionState = 'simulation' | 'connected' | 'connecting' | 'error';

export type GasStatusLevel = 'Safe' | 'Elevated' | 'Hazard';
=== Project cssNew folder/src/utils/esp32Api.ts ===
import { ControlCommand, TelemetryPacket, TrackerMode } from '../types/telemetry';

const clamp = (val: number, min: number, max: number) =>
  Math.min(max, Math.max(min, val));

function normalizeBaseUrl(baseUrl: string): string {
  return baseUrl.trim().replace(/\/+$/, '');
}

function parseMode(raw: unknown): TrackerMode {
  return raw === 'manual' ? 'manual' : 'auto';
}

/**
 * Fetches telemetry from the ESP32 `GET /api/telemetry` endpoint and normalizes numeric ranges.
 */
export async function fetchEsp32Telemetry(
  baseUrl: string,
  timeoutMs = 3500
): Promise<TelemetryPacket> {
  const cleanBase = normalizeBaseUrl(baseUrl);
  if (!cleanBase) {
    throw new Error('ESP32 endpoint URL is empty.');
  }

  const controller = new AbortController();
  const timer = window.setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), timeoutMs);

  try {
    const response = await fetch(`${cleanBase}/api/telemetry`, {
      method: 'GET',
      headers: { Accept: 'application/json' },
      signal: controller.signal,
    });

    if (!response.ok) {
      throw new Error(`HTTP ${response.status} ${response.statusText}`);
    }

    const data = await response.json();
    const ldr = data.ldr ?? {};

    return {
      temperature: Number(data.temperature ?? 0),
      humidity: clamp(Number(data.humidity ?? 0), 0, 100),
      gasPpm: Math.max(0, Math.round(Number(data.gasPpm ?? data.gas ?? 0))),
      panAngle: clamp(Math.round(Number(data.panAngle ?? data.pan ?? 90)), 0, 180),
      tiltAngle: clamp(Math.round(Number(data.tiltAngle ?? data.tilt ?? 90)), 0, 180),
      mode: parseMode(data.mode),
      ldr: {
        tl: clamp(Math.round(Number(ldr.tl ?? data.ldrTL ?? 0)), 0, 4095),
        tr: clamp(Math.round(Number(ldr.tr ?? data.ldrTR ?? 0)), 0, 4095),
        bl: clamp(Math.round(Number(ldr.bl ?? data.ldrBL ?? 0)), 0, 4095),
        br: clamp(Math.round(Number(ldr.br ?? data.ldrBR ?? 0)), 0, 4095),
      },
      rssi: Number(data.rssi ?? -65),
      uptimeSec: Math.max(0, Math.round(Number(data.uptimeSec ?? 0))),
      timestamp: Date.now(),
    };
  } finally {
    window.clearTimeout(timer);
  }
}

/**
 * Sends a mode or servo target override command to `POST /api/control` on the ESP32.
 */
export async function sendEsp32Control(
  baseUrl: string,
  command: ControlCommand,
  timeoutMs = 3500
): Promise<void> {
  const cleanBase = normalizeBaseUrl(baseUrl);
  if (!cleanBase) {
    throw new Error('ESP32 endpoint URL is empty.');
  }

  const controller = new AbortController();
  const timer = window.setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), timeoutMs);

  try {
    const response = await fetch(`${cleanBase}/api/control`, {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        mode: command.mode,
        panAngle: clamp(Math.round(command.panAngle), 0, 180),
        tiltAngle: clamp(Math.round(command.tiltAngle), 0, 180),
      }),
      signal: controller.signal,
    });

    if (!response.ok) {
      throw new Error(`Control command rejected (HTTP ${response.status})`);
    }
  } finally {
    window.clearTimeout(timer);
  }
}
=== Project cssNew folder/src/utils/simulation.ts ===
import {
  SimulationScenario,
  TelemetryPacket,
  TrackerMode,
} from '../types/telemetry';

export interface SimulationState {
  tick: number;
  panAngle: number;
  tiltAngle: number;
  targetPan: number;
  targetTilt: number;
  mode: TrackerMode;
  temperature: number;
  humidity: number;
  gasPpm: number;
  uptimeSec: number;
}

const clamp = (value: number, min: number, max: number) =>
  Math.min(max, Math.max(min, value));

const round1 = (value: number) => Math.round(value * 10) / 10;

export function createInitialSimulationState(): SimulationState {
  return {
    tick: 0,
    panAngle: 88,
    tiltAngle: 64,
    targetPan: 88,
    targetTilt: 64,
    mode: 'auto',
    temperature: 29.4,
    humidity: 54.2,
    gasPpm: 215,
    uptimeSec: 1420,
  };
}

/**
 * Computes sun position and environmental baselines for the selected demo scenario,
 * then simulates closed-loop LDR tracking (or manual servo hold) and 12-bit ADC irradiance.
 */
export function stepSimulation(
  prev: SimulationState,
  scenario: SimulationScenario,
  dtSec: number
): { nextState: SimulationState; packet: TelemetryPacket } {
  const tick = prev.tick + 1;
  const phase = tick * 0.14;

  let sunPan = 90 + Math.sin(phase * 0.45) * 48;
  let sunTilt = 68 + Math.cos(phase * 0.35) * 26;
  let ambientIntensity = 0.88;
  let tempTarget = 29.8 + Math.sin(phase * 0.2) * 1.4;
  let humTarget = 53.5 + Math.cos(phase * 0.25) * 3.2;
  let gasTarget = 220 + Math.sin(phase * 0.5) * 28;

  if (scenario === 'clear_noon') {
    sunPan = 90 + Math.sin(phase * 0.25) * 8;
    sunTilt = 82 + Math.cos(phase * 0.2) * 6;
    ambientIntensity = 0.96;
    tempTarget = 32.4 + Math.sin(phase * 0.2) * 0.8;
    humTarget = 46.0 + Math.cos(phase * 0.2) * 1.5;
    gasTarget = 195 + Math.sin(phase * 0.3) * 16;
  } else if (scenario === 'cloudy_drift') {
    sunPan = 65 + Math.sin(phase * 0.65) * 32;
    sunTilt = 52 + Math.cos(phase * 0.5) * 18;
    ambientIntensity = 0.42 + 0.22 * Math.sin(phase * 0.9);
    tempTarget = 25.1 + Math.sin(phase * 0.25) * 0.9;
    humTarget = 71.5 + Math.cos(phase * 0.3) * 2.8;
    gasTarget = 310 + Math.sin(phase * 0.4) * 35;
  } else if (scenario === 'gas_alert') {
    sunPan = 115 + Math.sin(phase * 0.3) * 14;
    sunTilt = 62 + Math.cos(phase * 0.3) * 10;
    ambientIntensity = 0.78;
    tempTarget = 36.2 + Math.sin(phase * 0.35) * 1.2;
    humTarget = 41.0 + Math.cos(phase * 0.2) * 2.0;
    gasTarget = 845 + Math.sin(phase * 0.85) * 145;
  }

  let panAngle = prev.panAngle;
  let tiltAngle = prev.tiltAngle;

  if (prev.mode === 'auto') {
    // Proportional slew toward the sun with smooth servo damping
    const panStep = clamp((sunPan - panAngle) * 0.28, -6, 6);
    const tiltStep = clamp((sunTilt - tiltAngle) * 0.28, -5, 5);
    panAngle = clamp(panAngle + panStep, 0, 180);
    tiltAngle = clamp(tiltAngle + tiltStep, 0, 180);
  } else {
    // Smoothly move to manual override target
    panAngle = clamp(panAngle + clamp((prev.targetPan - panAngle) * 0.45, -10, 10), 0, 180);
    tiltAngle = clamp(tiltAngle + clamp((prev.targetTilt - tiltAngle) * 0.45, -10, 10), 0, 180);
  }

  // Angular error drives differential readings across the 4 LDR quadrants
  const errX = clamp((sunPan - panAngle) / 45, -1, 1); // + => sun is toward Right (TR/BR)
  const errY = clamp((sunTilt - tiltAngle) / 45, -1, 1); // + => sun is toward Top (TL/TR)

  const baseAdc = 3820 * ambientIntensity;
  const jitter = () => (Math.random() - 0.5) * 70;

  // Cosine-like attenuation as panel faces away from sun
  const alignmentLoss = Math.min(0.45, (Math.abs(errX) + Math.abs(errY)) * 0.22);
  const centerAdc = baseAdc * (1 - alignmentLoss);

  const tl = Math.round(clamp(centerAdc * (1 - errX * 0.34 + errY * 0.34) + jitter(), 0, 4095));
  const tr = Math.round(clamp(centerAdc * (1 + errX * 0.34 + errY * 0.34) + jitter(), 0, 4095));
  const bl = Math.round(clamp(centerAdc * (1 - errX * 0.34 - errY * 0.34) + jitter(), 0, 4095));
  const br = Math.round(clamp(centerAdc * (1 + errX * 0.34 - errY * 0.34) + jitter(), 0, 4095));

  const temperature = round1(prev.temperature + (tempTarget - prev.temperature) * 0.25 + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.2);
  const humidity = round1(clamp(prev.humidity + (humTarget - prev.humidity) * 0.25 + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.4, 5, 99));
  const gasPpm = Math.round(clamp(prev.gasPpm + (gasTarget - prev.gasPpm) * 0.3 + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 14, 50, 2000));
  const uptimeSec = prev.uptimeSec + Math.max(1, Math.round(dtSec));

  const nextState: SimulationState = {
    ...prev,
    tick,
    panAngle: Math.round(panAngle),
    tiltAngle: Math.round(tiltAngle),
    temperature,
    humidity,
    gasPpm,
    uptimeSec,
  };

  const packet: TelemetryPacket = {
    temperature,
    humidity,
    gasPpm,
    panAngle: nextState.panAngle,
    tiltAngle: nextState.tiltAngle,
    mode: prev.mode,
    ldr: { tl, tr, bl, br },
    rssi: Math.round(-56 + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 6),
    uptimeSec,
    timestamp: Date.now(),
  };

  return { nextState, packet };
}

=== app/api/telemetry/route.js ===
import { Redis } from '@upstash/redis';
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';

const redis = Redis.fromEnv();

// POST: Receives sensor telemetry from ESP32 every 2 seconds
export async function POST(request) {
  try {
    const body = await request.json();

    const payload = {
      temp: Number(body.temp) || 0,
      hum: Number(body.hum) || 0,
      gas: Number(body.gas) || 0,
      pan: Number(body.pan) ?? 90,
      tilt: Number(body.tilt) ?? 90,
      ldr: Array.isArray(body.ldr) ? body.ldr : [1, 1, 1, 1], // [TL, TR, BL, BR]
      updatedAt: Date.now(),
    };

    await redis.set('solar_tracker:latest', payload);
    return NextResponse.json({ status: 'ok' });
  } catch (error) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Invalid JSON payload' }, { status: 400 });
  }
}

// GET: Serves the latest snapshot to the frontend dashboard
export async function GET() {
  const data = await redis.get('solar_tracker:latest');
  return NextResponse.json(data || { offline: true });
}

  variable: "--font-geist-sans",

  variable: "--font-geist-mono",
  subsets: ["latin"],
});

  title: "Create Next App",
  description: "Generated by create next app",
};

export default function RootLayout({ children }: LayoutProps<"/">) {

=== app/page.js ===
'use client';

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';

export default function SolarDashboard() {
  const [data, setData] = useState(null);
  const [isOnline, setIsOnline] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    const fetchTelemetry = async () => {
      try {
        const res = await fetch('/api/telemetry', { cache: 'no-store' });
        const json = await res.json();
        if (!json.offline && json.updatedAt) {
          setData(json);
          // Consider online if telemetry updated within the last 10 seconds
          setIsOnline(Date.now() - json.updatedAt < 10000);
        } else {
          setIsOnline(false);
        }
      } catch (err) {
        setIsOnline(false);
      }
    };

    fetchTelemetry();
    const interval = setInterval(fetchTelemetry, 2000);
    return () => clearInterval(interval);
  }, []);

  // Digital LDR modules output 0 (LOW) when bright light hits them
  const ldrLabels = [
    'Top Left (D27)',
    'Top Right (D26)',
    'Bottom Left (D25)',
    'Bottom Right (D33)'
  ];
  const ldrStates = data?.ldr || [1, 1, 1, 1];

  return (
    <main className="min-h-screen bg-slate-950 text-slate-100 p-6 md:p-10 font-sans">
      <div className="max-w-4xl mx-auto space-y-6">
        
        {/* Header & Status Indicator */}
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-slate-800 pb-4">
          <div>
            <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight">Dual-Axis Solar Tracker</h1>
            <p className="text-sm text-slate-400">ESP32 Telemetry & Environmental Monitor</p>
          </div>
          <span
            className={`px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-medium transition-colors ${
              isOnline
                ? 'bg-emerald-500/15 text-emerald-400 border border-emerald-500/30'
                : 'bg-rose-500/15 text-rose-400 border border-rose-500/30'
            }`}
          >
            {isOnline ? 'â— ESP32 Online' : 'â—‹ Waiting for ESP32'}
          </span>
        </div>

        {/* Environmental Telemetry Cards */}
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
          <div className="bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 rounded-xl p-5 shadow-sm">
            <p className="text-xs uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-400">Temperature (DHT22)</p>
            <p className="text-3xl font-semibold mt-2 text-amber-400">
              {data && data.temp !== undefined ? `${data.temp.toFixed(1)} Â°C` : '--'}
            </p>
          </div>

          <div className="bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 rounded-xl p-5 shadow-sm">
            <p className="text-xs uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-400">Humidity (DHT22)</p>
            <p className="text-3xl font-semibold mt-2 text-sky-400">
              {data && data.hum !== undefined ? `${data.hum.toFixed(1)} %` : '--'}
            </p>
          </div>

          <div className="bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 rounded-xl p-5 shadow-sm">
            <p className="text-xs uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-400">Air Quality (MQ135 Raw)</p>
            <p className="text-3xl font-semibold mt-2 text-purple-400">
              {data && data.gas !== undefined ? data.gas : '--'}
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Servo Gauges & 2x2 LDR Grid */}
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
          
          {/* Dual-Axis Servo Orientation */}
          <div className="bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 rounded-xl p-5 space-y-4 shadow-sm">
            <h2 className="text-sm font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-400">
              Servo Orientation (MG996R)
            </h2>

            {/* Horizontal Pan */}
            <div>
              <div className="flex justify-between text-sm mb-1">
                <span>Horizontal Pan (D19)</span>
                <span className="font-mono text-amber-400">{data ? `${data.pan}Â°` : '90Â°'}</span>
              </div>
              <div className="w-full bg-slate-800 h-2.5 rounded-full overflow-hidden">
                <div
                  className="bg-amber-400 h-full transition-all duration-300"
                  style={{ width: `${((data?.pan ?? 90) / 180) * 100}%` }}
                />
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Vertical Tilt */}
            <div>
              <div className="flex justify-between text-sm mb-1">
                <span>Vertical Tilt (D23)</span>
                <span className="font-mono text-sky-400">{data ? `${data.tilt}Â°` : '90Â°'}</span>
              </div>
              <div className="w-full bg-slate-800 h-2.5 rounded-full overflow-hidden">
                <div
                  className="bg-sky-400 h-full transition-all duration-300"
                  style={{ width: `${((data?.tilt ?? 90) / 180) * 100}%` }}
                />
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* 2x2 LDR Quadrant Array */}
          <div className="bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 rounded-xl p-5 shadow-sm">
            <h2 className="text-sm font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-400 mb-3">
              LDR Quadrant Array
            </h2>
            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
              {ldrLabels.map((label, idx) => {
                const isLit = ldrStates[idx] === 0;
                return (
                  <div
                    key={label}
                    className={`p-3 rounded-lg border text-center transition-colors ${
                      isLit
                        ? 'bg-amber-400/20 border-amber-400 text-amber-200'
                        : 'bg-slate-950 border-slate-800 text-slate-500'
                    }`}
                  >
                    <p className="text-xs font-medium">{label}</p>
                    <p className="text-sm font-bold mt-1">{isLit ? 'â˜€ LIT' : 'SHADED'}</p>
                  </div>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          </div>

        </div>

      </div>
    </main>
  );
}
